- **wsl-launcher**: Handles Windows-WSL integration — the Windows-side launcher, wsl.exe invocation, health-check polling, detached backend startup (setsid/systemd), port strategy, path translation. Use when a task touches launch scripts, startup lifecycle, or Windows interop. ([YaroslavSavchenk/ai-cli-application](https://github.com/YaroslavSavchenk/ai-cli-application/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wsl-launcher.md))
- **wsl-setup-checker**: Diagnoses WSL2 + NVIDIA + CycloneDDS environment issues — GPU passthrough, network interfaces, multicast, mirrored networking, locale, daemon health. Read-only. Use proactively when the user reports network or GPU weirdness, when topics don't appear, or when the bridge can't see the robot. ([pdeegan/ROS2-UnitreeG1-tutorial](https://github.com/pdeegan/ROS2-UnitreeG1-tutorial/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wsl-setup-checker.md) · ★1)
- **wsl2-expert**: Read-only WSL2 expert — the wsl.exe CLI, distro export/import, wsl.conf (per-distro) and .wslconfig (global VM) configuration, systemd in WSL2, NAT vs mirrored networking modes, and Windows/Linux interop and filesystem behavior. Does not modify files. ([psmfd/agent-framework-claude](https://github.com/psmfd/agent-framework-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/wsl2-expert.md))
- **wsl2-expert**: WSL2 specialist — `wsl.exe` CLI surface, per-distro `/etc/wsl.conf` and host-side `%UserProfile%\.wslconfig`, distro lifecycle including `wsl --export` / `wsl --import` rootfs packaging (the load-bearing path for distributing as a pre-populated tarball), `/etc/wsl-distribution.conf` OOBE contract, systemd-in-WSL2 enablement, NAT vs mirrored networking modes, Windows ↔ Linux interop and `WSLENV`, WSLg, and runtime diagnostics. Read-only advisor. Spawns isolated subprocess. ([psmfd/pi-config](https://github.com/psmfd/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/wsl2-expert.md))
- **wso2-trigger-onboarder**: Onboard a new low-code integration trigger to the WSO2 Integrator. Use when adding a trigger (event/webhook, database CDC, or message-broker/file source — e.g. shopify, mysql cdc, kafka, rabbitmq, ftp) backed by a Ballerina connector to the low-code editor. Drives changes across ballerina-language-server, vscode-extensions, and product-integrator, writes and runs LS tests, and opens three PRs. ([gayaldassanayake/claude-agents](https://github.com/gayaldassanayake/claude-agents/blob/HEAD/wso2-trigger-onboarder/agents/wso2-trigger-onboarder.md))
- **wsp-auto**: Default weight-space programmer. Given data or a task (tabular, images, or text) it picks the architecture and size and delivers a working network. It writes the weights directly where the task has structure it can reason about, uses gradient training where it doesn't, and makes surgical hand edits on top of either. It also improves existing networks (scikit-learn, PyTorch TorchScript / torch.export / pickled modules, npz, safetensors). Use via /wsp-build and /wsp-improve (mode auto), or whenever someone needs an ML model for a task. ([Mrazakos/weight-space-programmer](https://github.com/Mrazakos/weight-space-programmer/blob/HEAD/agents/wsp-auto.md))
- **wsp-hybrid**: Hybrid weight-space programmer. Builds or improves neural networks (MLPs, CNNs, text models, transformers, or imported PyTorch / scikit-learn models) by freely combining direct weight reading and editing with gradient training. It chooses the architecture and size itself. Use via /wsp-build or /wsp-improve with --mode hybrid, when the user wants gradient training and hand edits used together throughout. ([Mrazakos/weight-space-programmer](https://github.com/Mrazakos/weight-space-programmer/blob/HEAD/agents/wsp-hybrid.md))
- **wsp-pure**: Pure weight-space programmer. Builds a neural network for a dataset or task (tabular, images, or text), or improves an existing one, by reading and writing its weights directly. It never uses gradient training, following the MIT CSAIL "Sparks of Weight-Space Programming" rules, and chooses the network architecture and size itself. Use via /wsp-build or /wsp-improve with --mode pure, or when asked to hand-program or hand-repair network weights. ([Mrazakos/weight-space-programmer](https://github.com/Mrazakos/weight-space-programmer/blob/HEAD/agents/wsp-pure.md))
- **wsp-reviewer**: Dedicated reviewer for wsp ticket branches and PRs. Reads the wsp-review skill's checklist, reviews the diff against it, and posts findings as PR comments (or a ticket comment when no PR exists). Never edits code; never merges. Use for every ticket branch before the coordinator's merge pass. ([wsp-labs/wsp](https://github.com/wsp-labs/wsp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wsp-reviewer.md) · ★15)
- **wss-analyze**: Chooses its own read set against an open question and returns a plan the next rung works from — a design brief, a finding, or an artifact citation. The agent form of the dispatch ladder's Analyze rung. ([qupunto/wss](https://github.com/qupunto/wss/blob/HEAD/agents/wss-analyze.md) · ★1)
- **wss-design**: Takes a settled brief or plan and turns it into a design — an interface, a shape, a file-by-file sequence — without implementing it. The agent form of the dispatch ladder's Design rung. ([qupunto/wss](https://github.com/qupunto/wss/blob/HEAD/agents/wss-design.md) · ★1)
- **wss-execute**: Applies a finished design handed over as an artifact — file-by-file, without surveying the codebase or judging the design's correctness. The agent form of the dispatch ladder's Execute rung, the narrowest tool grant that can still write. ([qupunto/wss](https://github.com/qupunto/wss/blob/HEAD/agents/wss-execute.md) · ★1)
- **wss-feature-reviewer**: WSS-iOS-V2의 Feature 모듈(화면) 변경분을 전문 리뷰한다. View↔ViewModel 계약, UI 안전성(크래시·탭 트랩·디자인시스템 우회), 동작 정확성(비동기 경합·생명주기·도메인 값 매핑), Feature 코드 규약을 검사한다. "Feature 리뷰해줘", "이 화면 코드 봐줘", "PR 전 점검", "View/ViewModel 제대로 됐는지" 같은 요청에 사용. 기본 대상은 develop 대비 브랜치 diff 중 Projects/Feature/ 경로이며, 'staged'·'세션 변경분'·특정 모듈/파일 지정도 가능. 코드를 수정하지 않고 리포트만 낸다. ([Team-WSS/WSS-iOS-V2](https://github.com/Team-WSS/WSS-iOS-V2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wss-feature-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **wss-pr-reviewer**: WSS-iOS-V2의 PR 제출 전 변경분 전체를 레이어 가로질러 통합 리뷰한다. 특정 레이어에 특화되지 않고 Domain·Data·Core·UI·Feature·App을 함께 보며, 이번 변경이 레이어를 가로질러 의미가 맞는지(계약 양끝 일치·데이터 흐름·누락된 연결·이상 동작) + 각 레이어 핵심 계약·논리 버그를 검사한다. "PR 전 점검", "변경 전체 리뷰", "최종 코드 리뷰" 같은 요청에 사용. 기본 대상은 develop 대비 브랜치 diff 전체이며, 'staged'·'세션 변경분'·특정 경로 지정도 가능. 코드를 수정하지 않고 리포트만 낸다. ([Team-WSS/WSS-iOS-V2](https://github.com/Team-WSS/WSS-iOS-V2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wss-pr-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **wss-release-prep**: Prepares the material for a release — the version tier it proposes and why, the changelog entry text, and any release drift it finds. Invoked by --wss-release as the manifest's `WSS.agents.release`. Reads the release list, the changelog, the TODO list, the audit log, the cited roadmaps and the git history. It never tags, never commits and never publishes. ([qupunto/wss](https://github.com/qupunto/wss/blob/HEAD/agents/wss-release-prep.md) · ★1)
- **wss-rules-writer**: Writes rule rows to rulebook files in wss/rules/ — scoped strictly to that path, receiving all fields already decided, and validating each write with wss-rules-checkup.sh. The writer that fills the rulebook's judge files with rules passed to it from higher rungs. ([qupunto/wss](https://github.com/qupunto/wss/blob/HEAD/agents/wss-rules-writer.md) · ★1)
- **wss-survey**: Reads a named file set against a named method and returns a bounded verdict with `file:line` citations. Handed both its read set and its verdict format by the caller — it chooses neither, writes nothing, and decides nothing. The agent form of the dispatch ladder's Survey rung. ([qupunto/wss](https://github.com/qupunto/wss/blob/HEAD/agents/wss-survey.md) · ★1)
- **wss-v1-contract-extractor**: WSS-iOS-V2에서 운영 중인 V1(Team-WSS/WSS-iOS, UIKit·RxSwift)의 한 화면을 읽어 **동작 계약(behavior contract)**을 추출하고, V2가 그 각각을 유지/개선/삭제했는지 초안 분류해 해당 Feature 모듈에 `V1_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md`로 남긴다. ([Team-WSS/WSS-iOS-V2](https://github.com/Team-WSS/WSS-iOS-V2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wss-v1-contract-extractor.md) · ★3)
- **wt-auditor**: Audit worktracker integrity across multiple files with template compliance, relationship validation, and orphan detection ([geekatron/jerry](https://github.com/geekatron/jerry/blob/HEAD/skills/worktracker/agents/wt-auditor.md) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **wt-build**: Use this agent when WiredTiger C/C++ source or test code has changed and the CMake build needs verification. Typical triggers include verifying a fresh edit compiles before running tests, a pre-commit sanity check after touching files under `src/` or `test/` (Catch2, csuite, cppsuite, format, etc.), and confirming a newly added source or test file is wired into the build. ([wiredtiger/wiredtiger](https://github.com/wiredtiger/wiredtiger/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wt-build.md) · ★2.4k)
- **wt-condense**: Condense post-merge history into a clean linear sequence. ([godinj/godinj-dotfiles](https://github.com/godinj/godinj-dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/wt-condense.md))
- **wt-construct**: workspace-timing feature development ([guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions](https://github.com/guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions/blob/HEAD/.agents/subagents/agents/wt-construct.md))
- **wt-engineer**: Implements one plan unit to its Definition of Done in the ticket worktree and commits it by pathspec. Use for any unit whose plan `Owner:` is wt-engineer or has no domain engineer. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/wt-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **wt-explore**: workspace-timing context & design exploration ([guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions](https://github.com/guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions/blob/HEAD/.agents/subagents/agents/wt-explore.md))
- **wt-implementer-opus**: Use when implementing one increment requires judgment: derive a defect, choose among viable designs, or handle security, permission, or integration semantics. For a fully specified test-first change, use wt-implementer-sonnet instead. ([home-dev-lab/workflow-toolbox](https://github.com/home-dev-lab/workflow-toolbox/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/wt-implementer-opus.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **wt-implementer-sonnet**: Use when implementing one well-specified, test-first increment whose design and acceptance criteria are already settled. - **wt-lead**: Per-ticket coordinator for one wt-pack ticket. Use ONLY when started as `claude --agent wt-pack:wt-lead` and told a ticket id; it runs /wt-run. Never for design, code, review or user questions. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/wt-lead.md) · ★1)
- **wt-merge**: Merge a feature branch back into the current branch. ([godinj/godinj-dotfiles](https://github.com/godinj/godinj-dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/wt-merge.md))
- **wt-new**: Create a new git worktree for parallel feature development. ([godinj/godinj-dotfiles](https://github.com/godinj/godinj-dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/wt-new.md))
- **wt-product-planner**: Turns a ticket into a committed plan: scope, Definition of Done, test and QA brief, with an `Owner:` per unit. Use before any code is written; never implements or hands off to workers. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/wt-product-planner.md) · ★1)
- **wt-qa-auditor**: Verifies a built change as a user would, against the plan's Definition of Done. Reports pass/fail with evidence per DoD line; never fixes. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/wt-qa-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **wt-qa-lead**: Owns one ticket's QA on ONE stack and splits it across parallel wt-qa-auditor subagents by slice; owns rebuilds, batches failures to the lead, merges evidence into one gate B packet. Never fixes code. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/wt-qa-lead.md) · ★1)
- **wt-refactor**: workspace-timing refactoring & hardening ([guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions](https://github.com/guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions/blob/HEAD/.agents/subagents/agents/wt-refactor.md))
- **wt-review**: workspace-timing review & boundary analysis ([guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions](https://github.com/guilingzhouyi-creator/vscode-extensions/blob/HEAD/.agents/subagents/agents/wt-review.md))
- **wt-reviewer**: Use for an adversarial, read-only review of one plan or diff when correctness, missing cases, trust boundaries, and test strength need independent scrutiny before integration. ([home-dev-lab/workflow-toolbox](https://github.com/home-dev-lab/workflow-toolbox/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/wt-reviewer.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **wt-reviewer**: Read-only review of a plan or a diff with a lens set sized from what it touches, via wt-review. Returns findings and a verdict on a pinned sha; never edits. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/wt-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **wt-reviewer**: Reviews a merged build, or security and secrets files, against the spec and a hardening list; reports confirmed findings only (work-triage reviewer, Opus 5.5 high). Does not edit. ([LinusKon/work-triage](https://github.com/LinusKon/work-triage/blob/HEAD/skills/work-triage/agents/wt-reviewer.md))
- **wt-status**: Show the status of all git worktrees and feature branches in the current bare repo. ([godinj/godinj-dotfiles](https://github.com/godinj/godinj-dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/wt-status.md))
- **wt-sync**: Sync all feature worktrees by rebasing them onto the default branch. This pushes merged changes out to every active feature branch. ([godinj/godinj-dotfiles](https://github.com/godinj/godinj-dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/wt-sync.md))
- **wt-tech-lead**: Owns technical design, plan and diff review, and ship for a ticket; decides code questions, routes fixes to engineers, never implements. Use for design, review rounds and ship. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/wt-tech-lead.md) · ★1)
- **wt-verifier**: Verify work item acceptance criteria before closure ([geekatron/jerry](https://github.com/geekatron/jerry/blob/HEAD/skills/worktracker/agents/wt-verifier.md) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **wt-visualizer**: Generate Mermaid diagrams for worktracker hierarchies, timelines, status overviews, and dependency chains ([geekatron/jerry](https://github.com/geekatron/jerry/blob/HEAD/skills/worktracker/agents/wt-visualizer.md) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **wt-worker**: Executes a task brief inside an isolated git worktree. ([ms-jpq/ai](https://github.com/ms-jpq/ai/blob/HEAD/opt/codex/agents/wt-worker.md) · ★3)
- **wt-worker**: Builds modules, CLIs, tests and docs to a written spec (work-triage worker, Sonnet 5.5 medium). Give it the contract, the files it owns and its part of the spec. ([LinusKon/work-triage](https://github.com/LinusKon/work-triage/blob/HEAD/skills/work-triage/agents/wt-worker.md))
- **wt-worker-hard**: Like wt-worker for work dense with edge cases or with an approach to choose inside the spec, such as parsers, number formats, time, concurrency (work-triage worker, Sonnet 5.5 high). ([LinusKon/work-triage](https://github.com/LinusKon/work-triage/blob/HEAD/skills/work-triage/agents/wt-worker-hard.md))
- **wt_developer**: 並列 worktree 専用 developer。parallel-agents skill が isolation:"worktree" 付きで起動する用途。Stuck Signal のファイル名を task_id ベースにし、permission プロンプトを worktree 内でスキップする。 ([satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor](https://github.com/satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wt_developer.md) · ★1)
- **wt_refactorer**: 並列 worktree 専用 refactorer。parallel-agents skill が isolation:"worktree" 付きで起動する用途。permission プロンプトを worktree 内でスキップする。 ([satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor](https://github.com/satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wt_refactorer.md) · ★1)
- **wt_systematic-debugger**: 並列 worktree 専用 systematic-debugger。parallel-agents skill が isolation:"worktree" 付きで起動する用途。debug-analysis のファイル名を task_id ベースにし、permission プロンプトを worktree 内でスキップする。 ([satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor](https://github.com/satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wt_systematic-debugger.md) · ★1)
- **wt_tester**: 並列 worktree 専用 tester。parallel-agents skill が isolation:"worktree" 付きで起動する用途。test-report のファイル名を task_id ベースにし、permission プロンプトを worktree 内でスキップする。 ([satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor](https://github.com/satoh-y-0323/claude-code-conductor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wt_tester.md) · ★1)
- **wta-visualizer**: Specialist agent for the WTA Tennis Outfit Visualizer system — covers the full pipeline from YouTube video ingestion and color classification through the corrections editor UI and presentation mode. Use when working on the notebook, server routes, or WtaCorrections frontend. ([mmdonohue/zuzu](https://github.com/mmdonohue/zuzu/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wta-visualizer.md) · ★1)
- **wtf-change-reviewer**: Independent review of code in a fresh context — recent git changes, or a named subject reviewed as it stands. Diffs or reads the code, runs the test suite and the linter, then reports findings as Critical / Warning / Suggestion. Use when asked to review changes, review a branch, review how some area of the code works, or check work before committing or opening a PR. Read-only — it reports, it does not fix. ([wtfleming/dotfiles](https://github.com/wtfleming/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/Dotfiles/claude/agents/wtf-change-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **wtf-code-reviewer**: Strict reviewer for tuvix.js changes. Reads .claude/references/code-quality/* and CONTRIBUTING.md as rejection criteria, then checks the seven things no tool sees, including parity across the React, Vue, Svelte and Angular adapters. Use once after every Bug, Feature, Refactor or Critical implementation. Read-only. ([yasinatesim/tuvix.js](https://github.com/yasinatesim/tuvix.js/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wtf-code-reviewer.md) · ★23)
- **wtf-code-reviewer**: Strict code reviewer for lighthouse-guard TypeScript codebase. Reviews for Chrome leaks, async errors, rate limiting correctness, and TypeScript type safety. ([yasinatesim/lighthouse-guard](https://github.com/yasinatesim/lighthouse-guard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wtf-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **wtf-design-reviewer**: Early-cycle design review of work in progress, in a fresh context — is this change the right shape? Suggestion-only; every finding must name a concrete, smaller alternative and what it buys. Read-only — it reports, it does not fix. Dispatched by /wtf-design-review; not a defect hunter. ([wtfleming/dotfiles](https://github.com/wtfleming/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/Dotfiles/claude/agents/wtf-design-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **wtf-doc-reviewer**: Review a design doc, RFC, ADR or technical proposal in a fresh context — are its claims about the current system true, does the problem it solves exist, and is it ready to approve? Checks each factual claim against the code and, where reachable, production telemetry. Read-only — it reports, it does not edit the doc. Dispatched by /wtf-doc-review; not a code reviewer. ([wtfleming/dotfiles](https://github.com/wtfleming/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/Dotfiles/claude/agents/wtf-doc-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **wtf-lens**: Review a scope through one named lens only — security, tests, maintainability, resilience, performance, dependencies, compatibility, observability or ui. Dispatched several at a time by /wtf-code-review; not a general reviewer. ([wtfleming/dotfiles](https://github.com/wtfleming/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/Dotfiles/claude/agents/wtf-lens.md) · ★1)
- **wtf-prod-impact**: Assess whether a change would degrade production once merged and deployed, by reading the live system — what is deployed, how much traffic the changed path takes, what is already failing, and whether the change is gated. Builds failure trajectories and adjudicates each against telemetry. Dispatched by /wtf-prod-impact; not a code reviewer, and read-only against production. ([wtfleming/dotfiles](https://github.com/wtfleming/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/Dotfiles/claude/agents/wtf-prod-impact.md) · ★1)
- **wtf-reviewer**: Readability and maintainability reviewer — finds code that works but will confuse a developer reading it a month from now. Complements code-reviewer (correctness) and integration-reviewer (fit). ([NodeJSmith/Claudefiles](https://github.com/NodeJSmith/Claudefiles/blob/HEAD/agents/wtf-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **wtf-verify-adversary**: Propose the cases a verification pass should include that the author would not — the inputs that must be refused, and how they must be refused. Dispatched by wtf-code-verify with only a diff or a subject; not a general reviewer, and it runs nothing. ([wtfleming/dotfiles](https://github.com/wtfleming/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/Dotfiles/claude/agents/wtf-verify-adversary.md) · ★1)
- **wtfms-lab-definer**: Structures a researcher's resource description into VIRTUAL-LAB.md with domain-aware gap analysis and a resource-to-task mapping. Never interviews the researcher; reports missing details for the orchestrator to ask. Returns VIRTUAL LAB DEFINED or CHECKPOINT REACHED. ([iowarp/wtf-ms](https://github.com/iowarp/wtf-ms/blob/HEAD/agents/wtfms-lab-definer.md))
- **wtfms-literature-reviewer**: Conducts structured literature review for material science research from supplied papers first and the web second. Maps current knowledge, identifies gaps, proposes keyword refinements, and signals loop-back if the prompt needs revision. Never interviews the researcher. Returns LITERATURE REVIEW COMPLETE, LOOP BACK TO IDENTIFY-RESEARCH, or CHECKPOINT REACHED. ([iowarp/wtf-ms](https://github.com/iowarp/wtf-ms/blob/HEAD/agents/wtfms-literature-reviewer.md))
- **wtfms-research-explorer**: Synthesizes a researcher interview into candidate research prompts, then writes RESEARCH.md and STATE.md once the researcher has chosen. Never interviews the researcher directly. Returns CANDIDATES READY, RESEARCH IDENTIFIED, CHECKPOINT REACHED, or EXPLORATION INCONCLUSIVE. ([iowarp/wtf-ms](https://github.com/iowarp/wtf-ms/blob/HEAD/agents/wtfms-research-explorer.md))
- **wtfms-task-executor**: Executes individual research tasks by type — generates protocols, scripts, analysis code, literature summaries, or theoretical derivations — and verifies every output is on disk before reporting. Never interviews the researcher. Returns TASK COMPLETE, CHECKPOINT REACHED, or TASK BLOCKED. ([iowarp/wtf-ms](https://github.com/iowarp/wtf-ms/blob/HEAD/agents/wtfms-task-executor.md))
- **wtfms-workflow-planner**: Builds a research workflow from a selected template, the researcher's customizations, and the assumption answers the orchestrator already collected. Checks feasibility against VIRTUAL-LAB.md and orders tasks by dependency. Creates WORKFLOW.md. Never interviews the researcher. Returns WORKFLOW COMPLETE or CHECKPOINT REACHED. ([iowarp/wtf-ms](https://github.com/iowarp/wtf-ms/blob/HEAD/agents/wtfms-workflow-planner.md))
- **wtfp-argument-verifier**: Verify that completed section text actually delivers the claims, evidence, constraints, and connections promised by its plan. Execution summaries are navigation aids, not proof; verdicts must be grounded in the manuscript itself. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/antigravity/agents/wtfp-argument-verifier.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-citation-expert**: Find a precise, defensible set of sources for explicit manuscript claims and analyze whether the existing bibliography covers the approved argument. Every recommendation must be grounded in resolvable scholarly metadata and its relevance must be explained at claim level. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/claude/agents/wtfp-citation-expert.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-citation-formatter**: Audit bibliography integrity and produce deterministic corrections without touching the primary bibliography. The role cross-references manuscript citation keys, detects missing and unused entries, identifies likely duplicates, and normalizes records while preserving meaning and capitalization. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/gemini/agents/wtfp-citation-formatter.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-coherence-checker**: Evaluate the manuscript as a connected argument rather than a set of individually acceptable sections. Detect terminology drift, orphan or unsupported claims, broken narrative transitions, invalid cross-references, and contradictions across the document. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/copilot/plugins/wtfp/agents/wtfp-coherence-checker.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-outliner**: Turn the approved project brief into the structural foundation for an academic document. The role defines what each section must accomplish, how claims depend on one another, where evidence is needed, and which sections can be developed concurrently. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/antigravity/agents/wtfp-outliner.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-plan-checker**: Determine whether proposed section plans are likely to produce the intended academic outcome. Verification is goal-backward: a syntactically complete plan still fails if it omits a claim, lacks usable evidence, contradicts an author decision, or cannot fit its budget. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/claude/agents/wtfp-plan-checker.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-prose-polisher**: Improve clarity, rhythm, concision, and authorial voice without changing technical meaning, evidence, citation placement, or argumentative direction. Polishing is a constrained transformation, not a substantive rewrite. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/gemini/agents/wtfp-prose-polisher.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-research-synthesizer**: Investigate the literature needed to plan and write a specific section well. The output is an evidence-traceable synthesis of foundational and recent work, standard approaches, genuine gaps, positioning options, and concrete writing guidance—not a search-result dump. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/antigravity/agents/wtfp-research-synthesizer.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-section-planner**: Convert one approved section goal into small, executable writing plans. A plan is a precise writing contract: it connects claims, evidence, word budgets, sequencing, author decisions, and measurable completion criteria without pre-writing the section. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/copilot/project/.github/agents/wtfp-section-planner.agent.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-section-reviewer**: Review a section as an academic evaluator and produce prioritized, actionable feedback. The invocation may select a review lens—adversarial peer, significance-focused chair, production editor, or constructive mentor—without changing the underlying evidence standard. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/copilot/plugins/wtfp/agents/wtfp-section-reviewer.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **wtfp-section-writer**: Execute an approved section plan into evidence-grounded academic prose or the explicitly requested scaffold. Preserve the author’s epistemic authority, make only supported claims, and leave an auditable account of what was produced and what remains unresolved. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/copilot/plugins/wtfp/agents/wtfp-section-writer.md) · ★20 · 5 copies)
- **WTI-ROLES**: This directory is intended for WTI agent implementation code and related assets. ([WeTheInternet/wti-ai](https://github.com/WeTheInternet/wti-ai/blob/HEAD/ai/agents/WTI-ROLES.md))
- **wtp-validator**: Phase 9 execution - 6-layer WTP validation framework for pricing strategy ([ai-advanced-futures/cc-plugins](https://github.com/ai-advanced-futures/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/jtbd-market-discovery/agents/wtp-validator.md))
- **wukong-agent**: 父对话派 wukong-agent 写代码或打杂。动手前完整读 wukong-mode。不要派它去跑 playbook 本身。Use when the parent conversation dispatches wukong-agent to write code or do chores. Read wukong-mode in full first. Do not dispatch it to run a playbook. ([Acefake/s-skills](https://github.com/Acefake/s-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/wukong/agents/wukong-agent.md) · ★4)
- **wulan**: Wulan is the UX writer and localization specialist of the Agentic Designer studio. Wulan writes microcopy that shapes layout, reviews copy, translates, and runs the Localization Sync workflow (Strings (FE) sheet, keys, import-ready CSV, overflow checks). Use Wulan for copy, tone, translation, string keys, and localization batches. ([reyhan-ak-design/agentic-designer](https://github.com/reyhan-ak-design/agentic-designer/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic-designer/agents/wulan.md) · ★1)
- **wunderkind-architecture**: Architecture judgment for boundaries, operability, and engineering tradeoffs. ([grant-vine/wunderkind](https://github.com/grant-vine/wunderkind/blob/HEAD/codex-src/agents/wunderkind-architecture.md) · ★1)
- **wunderkind-ciso**: Security and compliance judgment for risk, controls, and escalation decisions. ([grant-vine/wunderkind](https://github.com/grant-vine/wunderkind/blob/HEAD/codex-src/agents/wunderkind-ciso.md) · ★1)
- **wunderkind-creative-director**: Creative and experience judgment for brand, interaction, accessibility, and visual direction. ([grant-vine/wunderkind](https://github.com/grant-vine/wunderkind/blob/HEAD/codex-src/agents/wunderkind-creative-director.md) · ★1)
- **wunderkind-legal**: Legal judgment for licensing, contracts, product claims, and regulatory posture. ([grant-vine/wunderkind](https://github.com/grant-vine/wunderkind/blob/HEAD/codex-src/agents/wunderkind-legal.md) · ★1)
- **wunderkind-marketing**: Marketing judgment for positioning, go-to-market, community, and developer adoption. ([grant-vine/wunderkind](https://github.com/grant-vine/wunderkind/blob/HEAD/codex-src/agents/wunderkind-marketing.md) · ★1)
- **wunderkind-product**: Front door for Wunderkind product judgment, specialist routing, and workflow choices. ([grant-vine/wunderkind](https://github.com/grant-vine/wunderkind/blob/HEAD/codex-src/agents/wunderkind-product.md) · ★1)
- **wurk-codebase-analyzer**: Analyzes codebase implementation details. Call the wurk-codebase-analyzer agent when you need detailed information about how a specific component works. As always, the more detailed your request prompt, the better. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-codebase-analyzer.md))
- **wurk-codebase-locator**: Locates files, directories, and components relevant to a feature or task. Call `wurk-codebase-locator` with a human-language prompt describing what you're looking for. Basically a "Super Grep/Glob/LS tool" - use it if you find yourself wanting to use one of those tools more than once. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-codebase-locator.md))
- **wurk-codebase-pattern-finder**: wurk-codebase-pattern-finder finds similar implementations, usage examples, and existing patterns that new work can be modeled after. It gives you concrete code examples based on what you're looking for. It's sort of like wurk-codebase-locator, but it does not only tell you where files are - it shows you the code. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-codebase-pattern-finder.md))
- **wurk-diff-critic**: Adversarial review of a finished branch's diff against the bead it claims to resolve, run by /wurk:mr's pre-request review round when a consumer names it in mr.review_agents. Reads the diff with fresh context and reports correctness findings - a behavior the bead asked for that the diff does not deliver, a change the diff makes that the bead did not ask for, an edge the new code mishandles, an error path that swallows what it should surface. It ranks each finding; the calling session decides what to do. Read-only: it runs git diff and git show, never edits, never commits. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-diff-critic.md))
- **wurk-docs-analyzer**: The project-document equivalent of wurk-codebase-analyzer. Use this subagent_type when you want a deep dive on one research document, implementation plan, or ADR. Not commonly needed otherwise. (Not for end-user documentation review; that is the write-doc/audit-doc family.) ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-docs-analyzer.md))
- **wurk-docs-locator**: Discovers relevant project documents - research documents, implementation plans, ADRs, and top-level design docs - wherever this project keeps them. Use it when you are in a researching mood and need to find out whether written material already exists on your topic. It is the project-document equivalent of `wurk-codebase-locator`. (Not for end-user documentation authoring; that is the write-doc/audit-doc family.) ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-docs-locator.md))
- **wurk-fleet-scout**: Read-only sweep of a fleet of repos. Builds the unified cross-repo ready-graph (bd ready + open beads per repo, the project's cross-repo link convention, package dependency edges from the fleet manifest) or verifies a status doc against live repo state. Returns structured data; never writes, claims, or pushes anything. Dispatched by /wurk:conductor. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-fleet-scout.md))
- **wurk-gate-reader**: Reads a COMPLETE failing quality-gate run and returns a triage: which stages failed, the root cause of each, which failures share one cause, and what to look at first. Use it whenever a gate comes back red and the output is more than trivially small - it absorbs the full output in its own context instead of the working session's, which is what makes honoring the never-truncate rule affordable. It diagnoses; it never fixes. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-gate-reader.md))
- **wurk-plan-critic**: Adversarial review of a drafted implementation plan, before it is presented. Reads the plan with fresh context - deliberate blindness to the authoring conversation is the point - and checks what a schema validator cannot: whether phases are genuinely independently committable and gate-verifiable, whether success criteria are actually verifiable, whether anything contradicts an accepted ADR, whether open questions survived, and whether the project's required sections are present. It reports findings; the authoring session judges them. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-plan-critic.md))
- **wurk-repo-worker**: Works exactly one bead (or one named integration-fix task) in one repo through the normal wurk pipeline under the policy block passed in by a conductor. Returns a structured result; never merges, never expands scope, never pushes the tracker. Dispatched by /wurk:conductor; a consumer whose campaigns need extra rules ships its own variant under a different name and its conductor dispatches that instead. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-repo-worker.md))
- **wurk-retro-reader**: Between-campaigns synthesis over the harness's own run history. Reads the campaign reports, journals, per-bead result files and the beads db, extracts concrete friction, clusters it ACROSS campaigns, and files an evidence-backed bead for every cluster that clears the recurrence bar. Read-only over the harness: it never edits a skill, agent, hook or script, never commits, never pushes. Spawned between campaigns by an operator or a scheduler, never by a running conductor mid-campaign. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-retro-reader.md))
- **wurk-test-critic**: Adversarial review of the tests a finished branch adds or changes, run by /wurk:mr's pre-request review round when a consumer names it in mr.review_agents. For each new or changed test it asks the one question a green run cannot answer - would this test fail if the code it covers were broken - and reports tests that would not: assertions that cannot fail, tests that exercise a mock instead of the subject, a sabotage note whose mutation would not turn the test red. It ranks each finding; the calling session decides. Read-only: it runs git diff and reads files, never edits, never runs the suite. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-test-critic.md))
- **wurk-web-search-researcher**: Do you find yourself wanting information you don't feel well-trained (confident) on? Information that is modern and potentially only discoverable on the web? Use the wurk-web-search-researcher subagent_type to find answers to your questions. It researches deeply and cites its sources. If you aren't satisfied the first time, re-run it with an altered prompt. ([riddler/wurk](https://github.com/riddler/wurk/blob/HEAD/agents/wurk-web-search-researcher.md))
